```
import { calculateShopDebt } from "../src/rules/utils/shopDebt.js";
import { installShopLawListeners, shopIncidentRecords } from "../src/rules/utils/shopLaw.js";

function addShop(world, shopkeeperId) {
  const roomId = world.create();
  world.add(roomId, RoomMetadata, {
    roomType: "shop",
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    w: 4,
    h: 4,
    shopkeeperId,
  });
  return roomId;
}

function addActor(world, x = 2, y = 2) {
  const actor = world.create();
  world.add(actor, Player, {});
  world.add(actor, Position, { x, y });
  world.add(actor, Inventory, { items: [], maxWeight: 999 });
  return actor;
}

function addTestItem(world, identity, name, value = 100) {
  const item = world.create();
  world.add(item, NamedIdentity, { identity, name });
  world.add(item, ItemInfo, {
    type: "item",
    slot: "",
    weight: 1,
    value,
    description: name,
    count: 1,
    bonuses: {},
    rarity: 1,
    rarityName: "common",
    affixes: [],
  });
  return item;
}

Deno.test("shop law records debt and incident when unpaid stock is thrown outside", () => {
  const world = new World({ seed: 9101 });
  installShopLawListeners(world);

  const shopkeeper = world.create();
  addShop(world, shopkeeper);
  const actor = addActor(world);

  const gem = addTestItem(world, "test_ruby", "Ruby", 250);
  world.add(gem, Unpaid, { shopkeeperId: shopkeeper, price: 250 });
  addToInventory(world, actor, gem);

  const escaped = [];
  const pursuit = [];
  world.on("shop:theft-escaped", (ev) => escaped.push(ev));
  world.on("shop:pursuit-requested", (ev) => pursuit.push(ev));

  world.add(actor, ThrowIntent, { itemId: gem, x: 6, y: 2 });
  throwSystem(world);

  assertEquals(calculateShopDebt(world, actor, shopkeeper), 250);
  const incidents = shopIncidentRecords(world, shopkeeper);
  assertEquals(incidents.length, 1);
  assertEquals(incidents[0].reason, "thrown_out");
  assertEquals(incidents[0].evidence, "seen");
  assertEquals(escaped.length, 1);
  assertEquals(pursuit.length, 1);
});

Deno.test("shop law converts a thrown unpaid stack unit into debt", () => {
  const world = new World({ seed: 9102 });
  installShopLawListeners(world);

  const shopkeeper = world.create();
  addShop(world, shopkeeper);
  const actor = addActor(world);

  const scrolls = buildCatalogItem(world, "scroll_mapping", { count: 3 });
  world.add(scrolls, Unpaid, { shopkeeperId: shopkeeper, price: 90 });
  addToInventory(world, actor, scrolls);

  world.add(actor, ThrowIntent, { itemId: scrolls, x: 6, y: 2 });
  throwSystem(world);

  const info = world.get(scrolls, ItemInfo);
  assertEquals(info.count, 2, "throw should remove one unit from the stack");
  assertEquals(calculateShopDebt(world, actor, shopkeeper), 90);
  const incidents = shopIncidentRecords(world, shopkeeper);
  assertEquals(incidents.length, 1);
  assertEquals(incidents[0].reason, "thrown_out");
});

Deno.test("shop law records an incident for unpaid potion consumption", () => {
  const world = new World({ seed: 9103 });
  installShopLawListeners(world);

  const shopkeeper = world.create();
  addShop(world, shopkeeper);
  const actor = addActor(world);

  const potion = world.create();
  world.add(potion, NamedIdentity, { identity: "potion_healing", name: "Healing Potion" });
  world.add(potion, Potion, {
    name: "Healing Potion",
    route: "oral",
    doses: 1,
    channels: [],
    effects: [],
    toxicity: null,
  });
  world.add(potion, ItemInfo, { type: "potion", slot: "", weight: 1, value: 10, description: "", count: 1, bonuses: {}, rarity: 1, rarityName: "common", affixes: [] });
  world.add(potion, Unpaid, { shopkeeperId: shopkeeper, price: 45 });
  addToInventory(world, actor, potion);

  world.add(actor, DrinkIntent, { itemId: potion, targetId: 0 });
  drinkSystem(world);

  assertEquals(calculateShopDebt(world, actor, shopkeeper), 45);
  const incidents = shopIncidentRecords(world, shopkeeper);
  assertEquals(incidents.length, 1);
  assertEquals(incidents[0].reason, "consumed");
  assertEquals(incidents[0].evidence, "arcane_mark");
});

Deno.test("shop law records teleport exit with carried unpaid stock", () => {
  const world = new World({ seed: 9104 });
  installShopLawListeners(world);

  const shopkeeper = world.create();
  addShop(world, shopkeeper);
  const actor = addActor(world);

  const gem = addTestItem(world, "test_emerald", "Emerald", 300);
  world.add(gem, Unpaid, { shopkeeperId: shopkeeper, price: 300 });
  addToInventory(world, actor, gem);

  const escaped = [];
  world.on("shop:theft-escaped", (ev) => escaped.push(ev));
  world.emit("moved", { id: actor, from: { x: 2, y: 2 }, to: { x: 8, y: 2 } });

  const incidents = shopIncidentRecords(world, shopkeeper);
  assertEquals(incidents.length, 1);
  assertEquals(incidents[0].reason, "teleport_exit");
  assertEquals(incidents[0].amount, 300);
  assertEquals(escaped.length, 1);
});

Deno.test("walking exit behavior still blocks unpaid carried stock before movement", () => {
  const world = new World({ seed: 9105 });
  installShopLawListeners(world);

  const shopkeeper = world.create();
  addShop(world, shopkeeper);
  const actor = addActor(world, 0, 2);

  const gem = addTestItem(world, "test_ruby", "Ruby", 250);
  world.add(gem, Unpaid, { shopkeeperId: shopkeeper, price: 250 });
  addToInventory(world, actor, gem);

  world.add(actor, MoveIntent, { dx: -1, dy: 0 });
  shopkeeperSystem(world);

  assert(!world.has(actor, MoveIntent), "shopkeeper should still remove the move intent");
  assertEquals(calculateShopDebt(world, actor, shopkeeper), 0, "blocked walking exit should not create extraction debt");
  assertEquals(shopIncidentRecords(world, shopkeeper).length, 0, "blocked walking exit should not record an escape incident");
});
```

```
    17	function normPoint(point) {
    18	  if (!point || typeof point !== "object") return null;
    19	  const x = Number(point.x);
    20	  const y = Number(point.y);
    21	  if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) return null;
    22	  return { x: x | 0, y: y | 0 };
    23	}
    24	
    25	function isInRoom(x, y, room) {
    26	  return (
    27	    x >= (room.x | 0) &&
    28	    x < ((room.x | 0) + (room.w | 0)) &&
    29	    y >= (room.y | 0) &&
    30	    y < ((room.y | 0) + (room.h | 0))
    31	  );
    32	}
    33	
    34	function itemIdentity(world, itemId) {
    35	  return String(world.get(itemId, NamedIdentity)?.identity || "").toLowerCase();
    36	}
    37	
    38	function itemName(world, itemId) {
    39	  const name = String(world.get(itemId, NamedIdentity)?.name || "");
    40	  if (name) return name;
    41	  return String(world.get(itemId, ItemInfo)?.name || "");
    42	}
    43	
    44	function inferSeverity(amount, reason) {
    45	  const value = Math.max(0, Number(amount || 0));
    46	  let severity = value >= 500
    47	    ? 4
    48	    : value >= 150
    49	    ? 3
    50	    : value >= 50
    51	    ? 2
    52	    : value > 0
    53	    ? 1
    54	    : 0;
    55	  const r = String(reason || "");
    56	  if (r === "teleport_exit" || r === "thrown_out" || r === "destroyed") {
    57	    severity += 1;
    58	  }
    59	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(5, severity));
    60	}
    61	
    62	export function findShopAt(world, x, y) {
    63	  const px = Number.isFinite(Number(x)) ? (Number(x) | 0) : 0;
    64	  const py = Number.isFinite(Number(y)) ? (Number(y) | 0) : 0;
    65	  for (const [roomId, room] of world.query(RoomMetadata)) {
    66	    if (String(room?.roomType || "") !== "shop") continue;
    67	    const shopkeeperId = normId(room.shopkeeperId);
    68	    if (!(shopkeeperId > 0)) continue;
    69	    if (isInRoom(px, py, room)) {
    70	      return { roomId: roomId | 0, room, shopkeeperId };
    71	    }
    72	  }
    73	  return null;
    74	}
    75	
    76	export function findOwnerShop(world, shopkeeperId) {
    77	  const owner = normId(shopkeeperId);
    78	  if (!(owner > 0)) return null;
    79	  for (const [roomId, room] of world.query(RoomMetadata)) {
    80	    if (String(room?.roomType || "") !== "shop") continue;
    81	    if (normId(room.shopkeeperId) !== owner) continue;
    82	    return { roomId: roomId | 0, room, shopkeeperId: owner };
    83	  }
    84	  return null;
    85	}
    86	
    87	export function isInsideShopRoom(world, x, y, shopkeeperId) {
    88	  const shop = findOwnerShop(world, shopkeeperId);
    89	  if (!shop) return false;
    90	  const px = Number.isFinite(Number(x)) ? (Number(x) | 0) : 0;
    91	  const py = Number.isFinite(Number(y)) ? (Number(y) | 0) : 0;
    92	  return isInRoom(px, py, shop.room);
    93	}
    94	
    95	export function recordShopIncident(world, spec = {}) {
    96	  const shopkeeperId = normId(spec.shopkeeperId);
    97	  const actorId = normId(spec.actorId ?? spec.actor);
    98	  if (!(shopkeeperId > 0) || !(actorId > 0)) return null;
    99	  if (!world?.isAlive?.(shopkeeperId)) return null;
   100	
   101	  const amount = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(Number(spec.amount || 0)));
   102	  const reason = String(spec.reason || "carried_out");
   103	  const incident = {
   104	    shopkeeperId,
   105	    actorId,
   106	    itemId: normId(spec.itemId),
   107	    amount,
   108	    reason,
   109	    evidence: String(spec.evidence || "ledger"),
   110	    severity: Number.isFinite(spec.severity)
   111	      ? Math.max(0, Number(spec.severity) | 0)
   112	      : inferSeverity(amount, reason),
   113	    createdTurn: Number.isFinite(spec.turn)
   114	      ? (Number(spec.turn) | 0)
   115	      : (Number(world.step || 0) | 0),
   116	    resolved: spec.resolved === true,
   117	  };
   118	
   119	  const incidentId = world.create();
   120	  world.add(incidentId, ShopIncident, incident);
   121	  attach(world, incidentId, shopkeeperId);
   122	
   123	  const rec = Object.freeze({ id: incidentId | 0, ...incident });
   124	  emitSafe(world, "shop:incident-recorded", rec);
   125	  return rec;
   126	}
   127	
   128	export function shopIncidentRecords(world, shopkeeperId = 0) {
   129	  const owner = normId(shopkeeperId);
   130	  const out = [];
   131	  if (owner > 0) {
   132	    for (const incidentId of children(world, owner)) {
   133	      const incident = world.get(incidentId, ShopIncident);
   134	      if (incident) {
   135	        out.push(Object.freeze({ id: incidentId | 0, ...incident }));
   136	      }
   137	    }
   138	    return out;
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  for (const [incidentId, incident] of world.query(ShopIncident)) {
   142	    out.push(Object.freeze({ id: incidentId | 0, ...incident }));
   143	  }
   144	  return out;
   145	}
   146	
   147	export function evaluateShopLawResponse(_world, spec = {}) {
   148	  const severity = Number.isFinite(spec.severity)
   149	    ? (Number(spec.severity) | 0)
   150	    : inferSeverity(spec.amount, spec.reason);
   151	  const reason = String(spec.reason || "");
   152	  return Object.freeze({
   153	    severity,
   154	    alarm: severity >= 4,
   155	    pursuit: severity >= 3 || reason === "teleport_exit",
   156	    escaped: reason === "teleport_exit" || reason === "thrown_out" ||
   157	      reason === "destroyed",
   158	  });
   159	}
   160	
   161	export function recordUnpaidExtraction(world, spec = {}) {
   162	  const actorId = normId(spec.actorId ?? spec.actor);
   163	  const shopkeeperId = normId(spec.shopkeeperId);
   164	  const itemId = normId(spec.itemId);
   165	  const amount = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(Number(spec.amount || 0)));
   166	  if (!(actorId > 0) || !(shopkeeperId > 0) || amount <= 0) return null;
   167	
   168	  const reason = String(spec.reason || "carried_out");
   169	  const debt = recordShopDebt(world, {
   170	    actorId,
   171	    shopkeeperId,
   172	    amount,
   173	    reason,
   174	    itemId,
   175	    identity: String(
   176	      spec.identity ?? (itemId > 0 ? itemIdentity(world, itemId) : ""),
   177	    ),
   178	    name: String(spec.name ?? (itemId > 0 ? itemName(world, itemId) : "")),
   179	    turn: spec.turn,
   180	  });
   181	  if (!debt) return null;
   182	
   183	  const incident = recordShopIncident(world, {
   184	    actorId,
   185	    shopkeeperId,
   186	    itemId,
   187	    amount,
   188	    reason,
   189	    evidence: spec.evidence || "ledger",
   190	    severity: spec.severity,
   191	    turn: spec.turn,
   192	  });
   193	  const response = evaluateShopLawResponse(
   194	    world,
   195	    incident || { amount, reason, severity: spec.severity },
   196	  );
   197	  const event = {

   199	    shopkeeperId,
   200	    itemId,
   201	    amount,
   202	    reason,
   203	    debt,
   204	    incident,
   205	    response,
   206	  };
   207	  emitSafe(world, "shop:debt-created", {

   213	    identity: debt.identity,

   216	  if (response.pursuit) emitSafe(world, "shop:pursuit-requested", event);
   217	  if (response.alarm) emitSafe(world, "shop:alarm", event);
   218	  return Object.freeze(event);
```

> AGENT

I can’t see `Entire` in this session. `tool_search` returns no `Entire` tools, and the installed plugins exposed here are GitHub and Google Calendar. The install-candidate list also doesn’t include `Entire`. If it is installed elsewhere, it is not currently callable from this Codex session.

On the model question: your instinct is mostly right. What we just built makes `ShopIncident` and `ShopDebt` very close structurally, especially for extracted-value cases.

The real distinction in the current implementation is:

`ShopDebt` = enforceable economic obligation attached to the actor.  
Example: unpaid potion consumed, thrown stack unit converted into value debt.

`ShopIncident` = shopkeeper memory/evidence attached to the shopkeeper.  
Example: teleporting out with carried unpaid stock records `teleport_exit` even though the item still has `Unpaid` and is not yet destroyed/converted into debt.

So the non-isomorphic case is carried unpaid goods escaping shop control: the debt amount can still be computed from `Unpaid`, while the incident records “how the shop knows this happened.” That is why `onMoved` records an incident but does not create `ShopDebt` for carried items in [shopLaw.js](/home/pjensen/Repos/JSHack/src/rules/utils/shopLaw.js:283).

That said, for `recordUnpaidExtraction()` cases, the two facts are currently duplicative: same actor, shopkeeper, item, amount, reason, turn, plus incident adds evidence/severity/resolved. We could absolutely collapse this by extending `ShopDebt` with `evidence`, `severity`, maybe `lastIncidentReason`, and only introduce a separate memory object later when we need multiple incidents per debt, non-debt suspicion, witness chains, or social/legal propagation.

Pragmatic read: `ShopIncident` is defensible as future shape, but right now it is a little ahead of the implementation. If you want the leaner version, I’d fold evidence/severity into `ShopDebt`, keep `Unpaid` for physical claims, and add separate incidents only when we add memory that is not payable debt.

```
# Shop Claim Follow-ups

## Speech bubble reliability

Shop claim dialogue currently routes through `npc:dialogue`, which is presented by speech bubbles. The speech bubble wiring drops NPC dialogue when the speaker is more than 8 tiles from the player.

Risk:
- Critical shopkeeper lines like payment demands, credit warnings, and refusal escalation can disappear in large shops or when the shopkeeper is away from the exit.

Follow-up:
- Let shop claim dialogue carry a priority/range hint, or special-case `source: "shop:claim-enforced"` and `source: "shop:unauthorized-use"` so those lines reach the player reliably.
- Add a test proving shop enforcement speech surfaces even when the shopkeeper is outside normal ambient chatter range.

## Stacked unpaid consumables

Unpaid potion use records debt before `consume`. For stacked potions, `consume` may decrement `ItemInfo.count` instead of destroying the entity, while `Unpaid` remains on the stack.

Risk:
- A single quaff may charge the full stack price.
- The remaining stack may still be marked unpaid, causing a second bill for value already charged.

Follow-up:
- Decide whether `Unpaid.price` means per entity, per stack, or per unit.
- If stacks can be unpaid, charge only the consumed unit and adjust the remaining unpaid price/count consistently.
- Add a regression test for unpaid stacked potion quaffing.

```
